### 65. SO5.2-P2-04

- **Standard:** SO5.2
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน

**Objective:** `SO5.2-P2-04` — มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.2-P2-04: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer / marking guidance:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Propose and sketch or describe one safe action at the marked school/community location that demonstrates “มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน”. Explain what evidence would show that the action helped. **Answer / marking guidance:** The action is practical for the named location, addresses the indicator, is safe for children and includes an observable success measure (for example, cleaner area, correct route/map, recorded observation or reduced waste).
### 66. SO1.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P3-01` — อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Before the lesson, prepare a three-sentence card from the curriculum-approved account, teaching, text, place or ceremony relevant to this indicator. Include (a) one named person/event/practice, (b) one accurate detail from the source, and (c) one teaching or purpose. Use the tradition and source selected for instruction; learners are not asked to disclose personal belief or participate in worship.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the source card. Name the person, event, teaching, practice, place or text that directly matches this indicator, and copy or point to one supporting detail. This assesses: “อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย”. **Answer / marking guidance:** A correct response identifies the item actually named in the teacher's verified source card and points to one accurate supporting detail. Accept oral, written or picture-supported answers. Do not accept an unsupported guess.
2. **Explain / compare:** Using the same source card, explain in one or two sentences what the identified teaching, event, practice or text means or why it is important for the exact learning target: “อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The explanation must match the source card and the indicator's requested concept (for example, a life example, teaching, role, meaning, respectful conduct or purpose). Award credit for one accurate idea and a source detail; accept equivalent wording.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Case: A class is preparing a display about the source card. A learner suggests an action that shows the idea respectfully in everyday school life. Describe one suitable action and explain how it connects to “อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย”. **Answer / marking guidance:** One feasible action is named and explicitly linked to an accurate idea from the source card. Accept different actions when the link is valid. Never grade a learner's faith, identity, worship or religious participation.

3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: แสดงความเคารพพระรัตนตรัยและปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P3-05: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Before the lesson, prepare a three-sentence card from the curriculum-approved account, teaching, text, place or ceremony relevant to this indicator. Include (a) one named person/event/practice, (b) one accurate detail from the source, and (c) one teaching or purpose. Use the tradition and source selected for instruction; learners are not asked to disclose personal belief or participate in worship.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the source card. Name the person, event, teaching, practice, place or text that directly matches this indicator, and copy or point to one supporting detail. This assesses: “แสดงความเคารพพระรัตนตรัยและปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer / marking guidance:** A correct response identifies the item actually named in the teacher's verified source card and points to one accurate supporting detail. Accept oral, written or picture-supported answers. Do not accept an unsupported guess.
2. **Explain / compare:** Using the same source card, explain in one or two sentences what the identified teaching, event, practice or text means or why it is important for the exact learning target: “แสดงความเคารพพระรัตนตรัยและปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The explanation must match the source card and the indicator's requested concept (for example, a life example, teaching, role, meaning, respectful conduct or purpose). Award credit for one accurate idea and a source detail; accept equivalent wording.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Case: A class is preparing a display about the source card. A learner suggests an action that shows the idea respectfully in everyday school life. Describe one suitable action and explain how it connects to “แสดงความเคารพพระรัตนตรัยและปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer / marking guidance:** One feasible action is named and explicitly linked to an accurate idea from the source card. Accept different actions when the link is valid. Never grade a learner's faith, identity, worship or religious participation.

- **Official indicator:** แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะหรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P3-03` — แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะหรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะหรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะหรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.2-P3-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Before the lesson, prepare a three-sentence card from the curriculum-approved account, teaching, text, place or ceremony relevant to this indicator. Include (a) one named person/event/practice, (b) one accurate detail from the source, and (c) one teaching or purpose. Use the tradition and source selected for instruction; learners are not asked to disclose personal belief or participate in worship.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the source card. Name the person, event, teaching, practice, place or text that directly matches this indicator, and copy or point to one supporting detail. This assesses: “แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะหรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer / marking guidance:** A correct response identifies the item actually named in the teacher's verified source card and points to one accurate supporting detail. Accept oral, written or picture-supported answers. Do not accept an unsupported guess.
2. **Explain / compare:** Using the same source card, explain in one or two sentences what the identified teaching, event, practice or text means or why it is important for the exact learning target: “แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะหรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The explanation must match the source card and the indicator's requested concept (for example, a life example, teaching, role, meaning, respectful conduct or purpose). Award credit for one accurate idea and a source detail; accept equivalent wording.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Case: A class is preparing a display about the source card. A learner suggests an action that shows the idea respectfully in everyday school life. Describe one suitable action and explain how it connects to “แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะหรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer / marking guidance:** One feasible action is named and explicitly linked to an accurate idea from the source card. Accept different actions when the link is valid. Never grade a learner's faith, identity, worship or religious participation.
### 76. SO2.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P3-01` — สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** A class has 4 shared storybooks, 6 learners who want to read, and one classroom rule: return a book before another learner borrows it. The teacher adds one short case detail matched to the indicator (family role, classroom duty, community decision, cultural practice, rights or public holiday).

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the case card. Identify the person, group, right, duty, rule, custom or decision-making step that is directly relevant to “สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น”. Give one detail from the case as evidence. **Answer / marking guidance:** Identify the relevant person/group/concept or step and cite the matching detail in the case. The response must fit the exact indicator, not merely repeat a general classroom rule.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain what would happen in this case if the relevant role, right, duty, custom or decision process in “สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น” were followed. Include one effect on another person or the community. **Answer / marking guidance:** State a plausible effect that follows from the case and correctly explains the indicator concept. Credit a clear cause-and-effect link and a relevant effect on others.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** The class must choose a fair action for the case. Propose one action, name who should take part or be responsible, and justify it using “สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The proposal is practical, respects the relevant rights/duties/customs or decision process, identifies the responsible participant(s), and gives a reason connected to the indicator. Accept alternative fair solutions with sound justification.

Propose one action, name who should take part or be responsible, and justify it using “ระบุบทบาทหน้าที่ของสมาชิกของชุมชนในการมีส่วนร่วมในกิจกรรมต่าง ๆ ตามกระบวนการประชาธิปไตย”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The proposal is practical, respects the relevant rights/duties/customs or decision process, identifies the responsible participant(s), and gives a reason connected to the indicator. Accept alternative fair solutions with sound justification.
### 81. SO2.2-P3-02

- **Standard:** SO2.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** วิเคราะห์ความแตกต่างของกระบวนการการตัดสินใจในชั้นเรียน โรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยวิธีการออกเสียงโดยตรงและการเลือกตัวแทนออกเสียง

**Objective:** `SO2.2-P3-02` — วิเคราะห์ความแตกต่างของกระบวนการการตัดสินใจในชั้นเรียน โรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยวิธีการออกเสียงโดยตรงและการเลือกตัวแทนออกเสียง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: วิเคราะห์ความแตกต่างของกระบวนการการตัดสินใจในชั้นเรียน โรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยวิธีการออกเสียงโดยตรงและการเลือกตัวแทนออกเสียง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: วิเคราะห์ความแตกต่างของกระบวนการการตัดสินใจในชั้นเรียน โรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยวิธีการออกเสียงโดยตรงและการเลือกตัวแทนออกเสียง

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.2-P3-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** A class has 4 shared storybooks, 6 learners who want to read, and one classroom rule: return a book before another learner borrows it. The teacher adds one short case detail matched to the indicator (family role, classroom duty, community decision, cultural practice, rights or public holiday).

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the case card. Identify the person, group, right, duty, rule, custom or decision-making step that is directly relevant to “วิเคราะห์ความแตกต่างของกระบวนการการตัดสินใจในชั้นเรียน โรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยวิธีการออกเสียงโดยตรงและการเลือกตัวแทนออกเสียง”. Give one detail from the case as evidence. **Answer / marking guidance:** Identify the relevant person/group/concept or step and cite the matching detail in the case. The response must fit the exact indicator, not merely repeat a general classroom rule.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain what would happen in this case if the relevant role, right, duty, custom or decision process in “วิเคราะห์ความแตกต่างของกระบวนการการตัดสินใจในชั้นเรียน โรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยวิธีการออกเสียงโดยตรงและการเลือกตัวแทนออกเสียง” were followed. Include one effect on another person or the community. **Answer / marking guidance:** State a plausible effect that follows from the case and correctly explains the indicator concept. Credit a clear cause-and-effect link and a relevant effect on others.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** The class must choose a fair action for the case. Propose one action, name who should take part or be responsible, and justify it using “วิเคราะห์ความแตกต่างของกระบวนการการตัดสินใจในชั้นเรียน โรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยวิธีการออกเสียงโดยตรงและการเลือกตัวแทนออกเสียง”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The proposal is practical, respects the relevant rights/duties/customs or decision process, identifies the responsible participant(s), and gives a reason connected to the indicator. Accept alternative fair solutions with sound justification.

### 83. SO3.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P3-01` — จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — limited-resource decision card:** A school club has 30 baht and 3 hours to prepare a class market. It needs price labels, reusable bags and a table display. The club cannot buy every possible material, so it must choose what is most necessary and avoid waste.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the numbers and details in the stimulus. Identify the good, service, resource, need, want, income, expense or saving that best demonstrates “จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต”. Show the relevant number or detail. **Answer / marking guidance:** The response correctly identifies the economic concept requested by the indicator and uses the exact supporting number/detail from the stimulus. For calculations, accept only mathematically correct totals and balances.
2. **Explain / compare:** Use the stimulus to explain one choice a learner or family can make that demonstrates “จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต”. Include the trade-off or result of that choice. **Answer / marking guidance:** The explanation names a realistic choice, uses the stimulus evidence and accurately describes its result (such as remaining money, a forgone purchase, a saved resource or a benefit to buyer/seller).
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Change one detail in the stimulus (for example, the amount of money or the available resource). Explain how the decision should change to continue meeting “จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต”, and give a reason. **Answer / marking guidance:** The revised decision follows logically from the changed constraint and is justified using the indicator concept. Accept more than one valid solution; check arithmetic and whether the response respects limited resources.

2. **Explain / compare:** Use the stimulus to explain one choice a learner or family can make that demonstrates “บอกสินค้าและบริการที่รัฐจัดหาและให้บริการแก่ประชาชน”. Include the trade-off or result of that choice. **Answer / marking guidance:** The explanation names a realistic choice, uses the stimulus evidence and accurately describes its result (such as remaining money, a forgone purchase, a saved resource or a benefit to buyer/seller).
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Change one detail in the stimulus (for example, the amount of money or the available resource). Explain how the decision should change to continue meeting “บอกสินค้าและบริการที่รัฐจัดหาและให้บริการแก่ประชาชน”, and give a reason. **Answer / marking guidance:** The revised decision follows logically from the changed constraint and is justified using the indicator concept. Accept more than one valid solution; check arithmetic and whether the response respects limited resources.
### 87. SO3.2-P3-02

- **Standard:** SO3.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกความสำคัญของภาษีและบทบาทของประชาชนในการเสียภาษี

**Objective:** `SO3.2-P3-02` — บอกความสำคัญของภาษีและบทบาทของประชาชนในการเสียภาษี
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกความสำคัญของภาษีและบทบาทของประชาชนในการเสียภาษี
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกความสำคัญของภาษีและบทบาทของประชาชนในการเสียภาษี

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.2-P3-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — limited-resource decision card:** A school club has 30 baht and 3 hours to prepare a class market. It needs price labels, reusable bags and a table display. The club cannot buy every possible material, so it must choose what is most necessary and avoid waste.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the numbers and details in the stimulus. Identify the good, service, resource, need, want, income, expense or saving that best demonstrates “บอกความสำคัญของภาษีและบทบาทของประชาชนในการเสียภาษี”. Show the relevant number or detail. **Answer / marking guidance:** The response correctly identifies the economic concept requested by the indicator and uses the exact supporting number/detail from the stimulus. For calculations, accept only mathematically correct totals and balances.
2. **Explain / compare:** Use the stimulus to explain one choice a learner or family can make that demonstrates “บอกความสำคัญของภาษีและบทบาทของประชาชนในการเสียภาษี”. Include the trade-off or result of that choice. **Answer / marking guidance:** The explanation names a realistic choice, uses the stimulus evidence and accurately describes its result (such as remaining money, a forgone purchase, a saved resource or a benefit to buyer/seller).
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Change one detail in the stimulus (for example, the amount of money or the available resource). Explain how the decision should change to continue meeting “บอกความสำคัญของภาษีและบทบาทของประชาชนในการเสียภาษี”, and give a reason. **Answer / marking guidance:** The revised decision follows logically from the changed constraint and is justified using the indicator concept. Accept more than one valid solution; check arithmetic and whether the response respects limited resources.

### 89. SO4.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO4.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P3-01` — เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Prepare three labelled evidence cards about the family, school or community topic named in the lesson: (A) an older photograph/object or first-person account, (B) a dated record or reliable written source, and (C) a present-day observation. Write the source and date on each card; do not invent dates or historical claims.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Examine evidence cards A–C. Identify the event, person, time, change, symbol or historical detail needed for “เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. State which card supports your answer. **Answer / marking guidance:** Name a detail that is explicitly supported by the cited card and identify the source correctly. If the indicator asks for sequence or calendar time, the order/date must agree with the evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two of the evidence cards. Explain one similarity, difference, sequence or cause-and-effect relationship that helps answer “เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The response accurately compares the chosen sources and states one supported relationship. Credit a correct time sequence or a plausible cause/effect only when supported by the evidence; distinguish evidence from opinion.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a two- or three-step timeline, labelled explanation or short oral-history summary that answers “เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Include the source label for each factual detail. **Answer / marking guidance:** The product includes the required number/order of steps or details, is consistent with the evidence cards, and identifies sources. Do not award factual credit for unsupported invented history.

3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a two- or three-step timeline, labelled explanation or short oral-history summary that answers “สรุปลักษณะที่สำคัญของขนบธรรมเนียมประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมของชุมชน”. Include the source label for each factual detail. **Answer / marking guidance:** The product includes the required number/order of steps or details, is consistent with the evidence cards, and identifies sources. Do not award factual credit for unsupported invented history.
### 93. SO4.2-P3-03

- **Standard:** SO4.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** เปรียบเทียบความเหมือนและความต่างทางวัฒนธรรมของชุมชนตนเองกับชุมชนอื่น ๆ

**Objective:** `SO4.2-P3-03` — เปรียบเทียบความเหมือนและความต่างทางวัฒนธรรมของชุมชนตนเองกับชุมชนอื่น ๆ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: เปรียบเทียบความเหมือนและความต่างทางวัฒนธรรมของชุมชนตนเองกับชุมชนอื่น ๆ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: เปรียบเทียบความเหมือนและความต่างทางวัฒนธรรมของชุมชนตนเองกับชุมชนอื่น ๆ

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.2-P3-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Prepare three labelled evidence cards about the family, school or community topic named in the lesson: (A) an older photograph/object or first-person account, (B) a dated record or reliable written source, and (C) a present-day observation. Write the source and date on each card; do not invent dates or historical claims.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Examine evidence cards A–C. Identify the event, person, time, change, symbol or historical detail needed for “เปรียบเทียบความเหมือนและความต่างทางวัฒนธรรมของชุมชนตนเองกับชุมชนอื่น ๆ”. State which card supports your answer. **Answer / marking guidance:** Name a detail that is explicitly supported by the cited card and identify the source correctly. If the indicator asks for sequence or calendar time, the order/date must agree with the evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two of the evidence cards. Explain one similarity, difference, sequence or cause-and-effect relationship that helps answer “เปรียบเทียบความเหมือนและความต่างทางวัฒนธรรมของชุมชนตนเองกับชุมชนอื่น ๆ”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The response accurately compares the chosen sources and states one supported relationship. Credit a correct time sequence or a plausible cause/effect only when supported by the evidence; distinguish evidence from opinion.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a two- or three-step timeline, labelled explanation or short oral-history summary that answers “เปรียบเทียบความเหมือนและความต่างทางวัฒนธรรมของชุมชนตนเองกับชุมชนอื่น ๆ”. Include the source label for each factual detail. **Answer / marking guidance:** The product includes the required number/order of steps or details, is consistent with the evidence cards, and identifies sources. Do not award factual credit for unsupported invented history.

### 97. SO5.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P3-01` — สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer / marking guidance:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Propose and sketch or describe one safe action at the marked school/community location that demonstrates “สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง”. Explain what evidence would show that the action helped. **Answer / marking guidance:** The action is practical for the named location, addresses the indicator, is safe for children and includes an observable success measure (for example, cleaner area, correct route/map, recorded observation or reduced waste).
### 98. SO5.1-P3-02

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** วาดแผนผังเพื่อแสดงตำแหน่งที่ตั้งของสถานที่สำคัญในบริเวณโรงเรียนและชุมชน

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P3-02` — วาดแผนผังเพื่อแสดงตำแหน่งที่ตั้งของสถานที่สำคัญในบริเวณโรงเรียนและชุมชน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: วาดแผนผังเพื่อแสดงตำแหน่งที่ตั้งของสถานที่สำคัญในบริเวณโรงเรียนและชุมชน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: วาดแผนผังเพื่อแสดงตำแหน่งที่ตั้งของสถานที่สำคัญในบริเวณโรงเรียนและชุมชน

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.1-P3-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “วาดแผนผังเพื่อแสดงตำแหน่งที่ตั้งของสถานที่สำคัญในบริเวณโรงเรียนและชุมชน”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer / marking guidance:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “วาดแผนผังเพื่อแสดงตำแหน่งที่ตั้งของสถานที่สำคัญในบริเวณโรงเรียนและชุมชน”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Propose and sketch or describe one safe action at the marked school/community location that demonstrates “วาดแผนผังเพื่อแสดงตำแหน่งที่ตั้งของสถานที่สำคัญในบริเวณโรงเรียนและชุมชน”. Explain what evidence would show that the action helped. **Answer / marking guidance:** The action is practical for the named location, addresses the indicator, is safe for children and includes an observable success measure (for example, cleaner area, correct route/map, recorded observation or reduced waste).

- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในชุมชน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในชุมชน

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.2-P3-06: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในชุมชน”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer / marking guidance:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในชุมชน”. **Answer / marking guidance:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Propose and sketch or describe one safe action at the marked school/community location that demonstrates “มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในชุมชน”. Explain what evidence would show that the action helped. **Answer / marking guidance:** The action is practical for the named location, addresses the indicator, is safe for children and includes an observable success measure (for example, cleaner area, correct route/map, recorded observation or reduced waste).
